# Untitled — component assignments

Use these named token assignments with the [theme foundations](untitled.md). Defaults and shared part references are already resolved; no Graphical runtime is required. Map each relevant part and state to the receiving app’s component styling API. Read only the component needed by the task.

Each appearance entry describes this theme’s actual edge placement and material effects for that mode and state. Compose the edge with the shadow stack; preserve the separate inner bevel when present. The shadows assignment is ordered, replaces the single legacy shadow choice, and includes editable inner highlights. An empty list means no shadows. Colors remain theme references; clipToBorder keeps an inner highlight inside the edge on an inset overlay.
